# 1442 new apps — 2026-11-14

Window: apps first found between 2026-11-13 18:31 UTC (19:31 Europe/Warsaw) and 2026-11-14 17:36 UTC (18:36 Europe/Warsaw) and released since 2026-11-12 18:31 UTC (19:31 Europe/Warsaw), plus apps found earlier whose release date arrived in that span.
